<script>
		$(document).ready(function() {
		    
		});

		function toggleCourses(contentId, arrowId, button) {
			var content = document.getElementById(contentId);
			var arrow = document.getElementById(arrowId);

			if (content.classList.contains("hide")) {
				content.classList.remove("hide"); // Show content
				arrow.classList.remove("fa-chevron-down");
				arrow.classList.add("fa-chevron-up"); // Change to up arrow
				
				// Update ARIA attributes
				button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
				var labelText = button.getAttribute('aria-label');
				labelText = labelText.replace('Collapsed. Press Enter or Space to expand.', 'Expanded. Press Enter or Space to collapse.');
				button.setAttribute('aria-label', labelText);
				
				// Announce state change to screen readers
				var statusEl = document.getElementById('course-accordion-status');
				if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = 'Expanded';
				
				// DataLayer tracking for open
				if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
					var btnText = button.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
					dataLayer.push({"event":"collapse","action":"open","name":"onclick","type":"click","region":"main content","section":"requirement","text":btnText,"component":"courses"});
				}
			} else {
				content.classList.add("hide"); // Hide content
				arrow.classList.remove("fa-chevron-up");
				arrow.classList.add("fa-chevron-down"); // Change to down arrow
				
				// Update ARIA attributes
				button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
				var labelText = button.getAttribute('aria-label');
				labelText = labelText.replace('Expanded. Press Enter or Space to collapse.', 'Collapsed. Press Enter or Space to expand.');
				button.setAttribute('aria-label', labelText);
				
				// Announce state change to screen readers
				var statusEl = document.getElementById('course-accordion-status');
				if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = 'Collapsed';
				
				// DataLayer tracking for close
				if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
					var btnText = button.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
					dataLayer.push({"event":"collapse","action":"close","name":"onclick","type":"click","region":"main content","section":"requirement","text":btnText,"component":"courses"});
				}
			}
		}
		
		function toggleAllCourses() {
		    var allContent = document.querySelectorAll('.courseListContent');
		    var allArrows = document.querySelectorAll('.arrow');
		    var allButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.toggle-btn');
		    var toggleButton = document.getElementById('toggleAllBtn');
		    var toggleIcon = document.getElementById('toggleAllIcon');
		    var expanding = toggleButton.innerText.includes("Expand");

		    allContent.forEach(content => {
		        if (expanding) {
		            content.classList.remove("hide"); // Show all
		        } else {
		            content.classList.add("hide"); // Hide all
		        }
		    });

		    allArrows.forEach(arrow => {
		        if (expanding) {
		            arrow.classList.remove("fa-chevron-down");
		            arrow.classList.add("fa-chevron-up");
		        } else {
		            arrow.classList.remove("fa-chevron-up");
		            arrow.classList.add("fa-chevron-down");
		        }
		    });
		    
		    // Update ARIA attributes on all individual accordion buttons
		    allButtons.forEach(button => {
		        if (expanding) {
		            button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
		            var labelText = button.getAttribute('aria-label');
		            if (labelText) {
		                labelText = labelText.replace('Collapsed. Press Enter or Space to expand.', 'Expanded. Press Enter or Space to collapse.');
		                button.setAttribute('aria-label', labelText);
		            }
		        } else {
		            button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
		            var labelText = button.getAttribute('aria-label');
		            if (labelText) {
		                labelText = labelText.replace('Expanded. Press Enter or Space to collapse.', 'Collapsed. Press Enter or Space to expand.');
		                button.setAttribute('aria-label', labelText);
		            }
		        }
		    });

		    // Toggle button text and icon
		    if (expanding) {
		        toggleButton.innerHTML = '<i id="toggleAllIcon" class="fa fa-chevron-up"></i>Collapse All Course lists';
		        toggleButton.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Collapse all course lists');
		        // Announce to screen readers
		        var statusEl = document.getElementById('course-accordion-status');
		        if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = 'All course lists expanded';
		        // DataLayer tracking
		        if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
		            dataLayer.push({"event":"collapse","action":"open","name":"onclick","type":"click","region":"main content","section":"2026-2027 catalog year","text":"expand all course lists","component":"courses"});
		        }
		    } else {
		        toggleButton.innerHTML = '<i id="toggleAllIcon" class="fa fa-chevron-down"></i>Expand All Course lists';
		        toggleButton.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Expand all course lists');
		        // Announce to screen readers
		        var statusEl = document.getElementById('course-accordion-status');
		        if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = 'All course lists collapsed';
		        // DataLayer tracking
		        if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
		            dataLayer.push({"event":"collapse","action":"close","name":"onclick","type":"click","region":"main content","section":"2026-2027 catalog year","text":"collapse all course lists","component":"courses"});
		        }
		    }
		}

	</script>
	

	<script>
	// DataLayer tracking for course links (ttCourse), elective links, and notes links
	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
		var parent = document.getElementById('checksheet-course-table');
		if (!parent) return;
		parent.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
			var link = e.target.closest('a');
			if (!link) return;
			
			// Skip toggle buttons (course list expanders) which have their own collapse tracking
			if (link.closest('.toggle-btn') || link.id === 'toggleAllBtn') return;
			
			var linkText = link.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
			var linkUrl = link.href || '';
			
			// Determine type: mailto = external, asu.edu = internal, else external
			var linkType = 'internal link';
			if (linkUrl.startsWith('mailto:')) {
				linkType = 'external link';
			} else if (linkUrl && !linkUrl.includes('asu.edu')) {
				linkType = 'external link';
			}
			
			if (typeof dataLayer !== 'undefined') {
				dataLayer.push({
					"event": "link",
					"action": "click",
					"name": "onclick",
					"type": linkType,
					"region": "main content",
					"section": "requirement",
					"text": linkText,
					"component": "courses"
				});
			}
		});
	});
	</script>

    // Add ESC key functionality to close dropdown menus
    $(document).ready(function() {
      $(document).on('keydown', function(e) {
        if (e.keyCode === 27) { // ESC key
          console.log('ESC pressed - closing ASU header dropdowns');
          // Close any open dropdowns in the ASU header navigation
          $('#asu-header-nav .navlist .navlink').removeClass('open');
          $('#asu-header-nav .dropdown-menu').hide();
          // Also try to close any Bootstrap dropdowns
          $('.dropdown.open').removeClass('open');
          $('.dropdown-menu').hide();
          // Try ASU components library specific classes
          $('.navlink.open').removeClass('open');
          $('.nav-dropdown.open').removeClass('open');
        }
      });
    });

			<script>
				$(function() {
					var showTxt = 'Show Course List(s)/Track Group(s)';
					var hideTxt = 'Hide Course List(s)/Track Group(s)';
	// 				element.prev('#trackGroupHeader').html(hideTxt);
					convertTrackGroup();
					
					$('.mobile-toggle').closest('.mobileRow').on('click', function() {
						if($(this).find('.mobile-toggle').is(':visible')){
							$(this).next('.mobile-toggle-content').slideToggle('fast');
							var element = $(this).find('.mobile-toggle');
							if(element.find('.chevron-icon').hasClass('fa-chevron-down')){
								element.find('.chevron-icon').removeClass('fa-chevron-down');
		         				element.find('.chevron-icon').addClass('fa-chevron-up');
		         				element.find('.chevron-icon').addClass('red-font');
								element.closest('.mobileCell').addClass('red-font');
								element.prev('.mobile-outputMessage').addClass('red-font');
							}else{
								element.find('.chevron-icon').removeClass('fa-chevron-up');
		         				element.find('.chevron-icon').addClass('fa-chevron-down');
		         				element.find('.chevron-icon').removeClass('red-font');
								element.closest('.mobileCell').removeClass('red-font');
								element.prev('.mobile-outputMessage').removeClass('red-font');
							}
						}
					});
					
					$('.track-toggle').closest('#trackGroupHeader').on('click', function() {
						if($(this).find('.track-toggle').is(':visible')){
							$(this).next('#mobileTrackGroup').slideToggle('fast');
							var element = $(this).find('.track-toggle');
							if(element.text() == String.fromCharCode(9650)){
								element.html('&#9660;');
								element.removeClass('red-font');
								element.closest('#trackGroupHeader').removeClass('red-font');
							}else{
								element.html('&#9650;');
								element.addClass('red-font');
								element.closest('#trackGroupHeader').addClass('red-font');						
							}
						}
					});
					
					$('.mobile-outputMessage a').each(function(){
						var $this = $(this);
						var catalogUrl = $this.attr('href');
						var tooltipUrl = $this.attr('rel');
						var contents = $this.html();
						
						if(tooltipUrl){
							/* var url = '/major-map/courseInfo?tooltipUrl='+tooltipUrl+'&catalogUrl='+catalogUrl; */
							var url = catalogUrl
							$this.html('<a href="'+url+'" target="_blank">'+contents+'</a>');
						}else if(catalogUrl){
							var url = catalogUrl;
							if(url=="#trackgroup"){
								$this.html('<a href="#mobileTrackGroup">'+contents+'</a>');
							}
							else{
								$this.html('<a href="'+url+'" target="_blank">'+contents+'</a>');
							}
							
						}
						
						$this.contents().unwrap();
					});
					
					$('.termNotes').each(function(){
						if($(this).children().length && !$.trim($(this).text())){
							$(this).closest('.mobileRow').hide();
						}
					});
					
					$('#requestInfoBtn').on('click', function(){
						window.open("https://admission.asu.edu/request-info?prog=UGBA&plan=BAACCBS&name=Accountancy&contact=wpcareyacc@asu.edu&location=ONLNE");
					});
					
					
					$('#degreeDescrBtn').on('click', function(){
						window.close();
					});
				});
				
				function convertTrackGroup(){
					var html = '<div class="mobileTable table">';
					var isFirstTrackGroup = true;
					$('#mobileTrackGroup li').each(function(){
						var $this = $(this);
						var contents = $this.html();
						var firstTitleHtml = ''
							+'<div class="mobileRow row">'
							+	'<div class="mobileCell cell">'
							+		'<div class="termTitle">'+contents+'<span class="mobile-toggle right-align" style="margin-left: 16px;"><i class="fa fa-chevron-down chevron-icon" aria-hidden="true"></i></span></div>'
							+	'</div>'
							+'</div>'
							+'<div class="mobileRow row mobile-toggle-content">' //starts mobileTermTbl wrap
							+	'<div class="mobileCell cell">'
							+		'<div class="mobileTermTbl mobileTable table">';
							
						var titleHtml = ''
							+		'</div>' //closes mobileTermTbl
							+	'</div>' //closes mobileCell
							+'</div>' //closes MobileRow
							+firstTitleHtml;
						
						if($this.hasClass('listTitle')){
							if(isFirstTrackGroup){
								html += firstTitleHtml;
								isFirstTrackGroup = false;
							}else{
								html += titleHtml;
							}
						}else{
							html += '<div class="mobileRow row"><div class="mobileCell cell"><div class="mobile-outputMessage">'+contents+'</div></div></div>';
						}
					});
					html += ''
						+			'</div>' //closes mobileTermTbl
						+		'</div>' //closes mobileCell
						+	'</div>' //closes mobileRow
						+'</div>'; //closes mobileTable
					$('#mobileTrackGroup').find('.trackGroupDiv').html(html).contents().unwrap();		
				};
				
				$(document).ready(function() {
					if($(".tt_Apply").hasClass("btn-sm")) {
						$(".tt_Apply").removeClass("btn-sm");
					}
					
					if($(".tt_Apply").hasClass("btn-block")) {
						$(".tt_Apply").removeClass("btn-block");
					}
				});
			</script>
		
	</head>

    // Add ESC key functionality to close dropdown menus
    $(document).ready(function() {
      $(document).on('keydown', function(e) {
        if (e.keyCode === 27) { // ESC key
          console.log('ESC pressed - closing ASU header dropdowns');
          // Close any open dropdowns in the ASU header navigation
          $('#asu-header-nav .navlist .navlink').removeClass('open');
          $('#asu-header-nav .dropdown-menu').hide();

          $('.dropdown.open').removeClass('open');
          $('.dropdown-menu').hide();
          // Try ASU components library specific classes
          $('.navlink.open').removeClass('open');
          $('.nav-dropdown.open').removeClass('open');
        }
      });
    });

                <h1><span id="MainContent_lblTitle">Undergraduate Degree</span></h1>
                <h2>
                    <span id="MainContent_lblProg">Accounting</span></h2>

                
                <div class="row test-contentCourseDesc">
                    <div class="col-md-3"><strong>Program:</strong></div>
                    <div id="MainContent_tdProgram" class="col-md-7">Accounting</div>

                <div class="row test-contentCourseDesc">

                    <div id="MainContent_tdMajor" class="col-md-7">ACCOUN_BS1</div>

                <div class="row test-contentCourseDesc">
                    <div class="col-md-3"><strong>College:</strong></div>
                    <div id="MainContent_tdCollDesc" class="col-md-7">Eli Broad College of Business</div>
                </div>
                <div id="MainContent_trDept" class="row test-contentCourseDesc">
                    <div class="col-md-3"><strong>Department:</strong></div>
                    <div id="MainContent_tdDeptDesc" class="col-md-7">Accounting and Information Systems</div>
                </div>
                    </div>


                <br />
                <br />


                <div id="MainContent_divOrigID">
                    <b><em>Excerpt from the official Academic Programs Catalog:</em></b>
                    <br />
                    <br />
                    <div id="MainContent_divDesc" class="ClearTables">
                        Listed below are the approved requirements for the program from the official Academic Programs Catalog.
	            <br />
                        Students must consult their advisors to learn which specific requirements apply to their degree programs.
	            <br />
                    <br><br/><div class="panel panel-default"><div class="panel-heading"><h3 class="panel-title">The Eli Broad College of Business and The Eli Broad Graduate School of Management</h3><h4 class="panel-title">Department of Accounting and Information Systems</h4><h5 class="panel-title">Undergraduate Program</h5><h6 class="panel-title">Accounting</h6></div><div class="panel-body"><div style='padding-bottom: 2px;text-align:right'><a href='Text.aspx?Section=117#s1266'>View this text within the context of the catalog.</a></div><br/><p>The Department of Accounting and Information Systems provides a Bachelor of Science degree with a major in accounting.&nbsp; The program provides an excellent foundation in accounting.&nbsp; Students, who complete the program along with the offered government and not-for-profit accounting course, meet the State of Michigan educational requirements to sit for the Certified Public Accountant (CPA) exam in Michigan.&nbsp;</p>

<p><strong>Requirements for the Bachelor of Science&nbsp;Degree in Accounting</strong></p>

<ol>
	<li>The graduation requirements of the University as described in the <a class="link" target="_new" class=link  target='_new' class=link href="https://reg.msu.edu/AcademicPrograms/Text.aspx?Section=110" target="_new"><em>Undergraduate Education</em></a> section of this catalog; 120 credits, including general elective credits, are required for the Bachelor of Science degree in Accounting.<br /><br />The completion of Mathematics 103 and 124 [referenced in item 2. below] may also satisfy the University mathematics requirement.<br /><br />The University&#39;s Tier II writing requirement for the Accounting major is met by completing Accounting 301 and 331.&nbsp; Those courses are referenced in item 3. a. below.</li>
	<li>The requirements of The Eli Broad College of Business for the Bachelor of Science degree in the majors that comprise the Business Administration Programs.</li>
	<li>The following requirements for the major:

        <div class="rvt-row rvt-m-bottom-xl">
            <div class="rvt-cols-12-md">
                <p>This academic degree requirements section is information only for Indiana University Bloomington (IUB) Kelley School of Business undergraduate students pursuing the Bachelor of Science in Business (B.S.B.). Degree requirements can change from year to year. You must follow the requirements outlined in the bulletin published in the year you first matriculated (enrolled) as a degree-seeking Kelley student at IUB. The new academic bulletin year starts in the summer (i.e. students who matriculate to IUB in a summer term follow the same degree requirements as students who start during the fall semester of that year). IUB Kelley Policies are not specific to a student's matriculation academic year. Once a policy is implemented or updated, it is immediately binding for all current, future, and previous students. For instance, if IUB changes a campus withdrawal policy, it applies to current students, future students, and any previously enrolled students that return later to complete coursework. For questions regarding which bulletin to use, please consult the Kelley Academic Advising Office, <a href="mailto:busadv@iu.edu" aria-label="busadv@iu.edu">busadv@iu.edu</a>. To access Kelley School academic bulletins for other years, please see the <a href="https://bulletins.iu.edu/iub/#kelley-ugrad" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="IU Bloomington Bulletins (opens in new window)">IU Bloomington Bulletins<span class="rvt-sr-only"> (opens in new window)</span></a> site.</p>
            </div>
        </div>
